The top-200 list is stored in corpus.json (`topUnresolved`). Typical misses: proper names and foreign words (not in VESUM), Old Rus' lexemes with no modern descendant, scribal variants and spellings that no rule maps to a VESUM form.

### 2.1 Epoch paradigms (SPEC 50)
Noun lemmas of the lexicon; each token linked to such a lemma is placed in a grammatical cell of its shev epoch by the tag of its own source (EP-2.1), or dropped. **Tagged** = placed + conflict + underspecified (EP-3.7); **untagged** tokens have no tag source (no TOROT morph, no Stanza output, not a modern direct link) and are not part of N. A token with an ambiguous tag is placed in each candidate cell (≤ 4), so cell counts exceed placed tokens. A cell with no token is `unknown`, never "not used" (EP-3.8).

**Placement of the test lemmas (SPEC 61 FX-6)** — tokens (not token × epoch) by outcome. ## 4. Lexical statistics (M-5)

Methods — ipm: DWDS <https://www.dwds.de/d/plot>: «Kategorisierungsfunktion zur Berechnung des Nenners für die Ergebnis-Skalierung pro Million Tokens». Frequency class HK: WLIST <https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Word_list>: «The most common item belongs to frequency class 0 (zero) and any item that is approximately half as frequent belongs in class 1.» (f_max = 36303, the most frequent lemma). Gries DP: GRIES <https://stgries.info/research/2008_STG_Dispersion_IJCL.pdf>: «DP = ½ Σ |y_i/AF − n_i/N| (0 = perfectly even, 1 = concentrated)». G²/Log Ratio: UCREL <https://ucrel.lancs.ac.uk/llwizard.html>: «G² = 2·(a·ln(a/E1) + b·ln(b/E2)); critical values 3.84 (p<.05), 6.63, 10.83, 15.13». Smoothing: DWDS <https://www.dwds.de/d/plot>: «Fensterbreite für gleitenden Mittelwert (Glättung), als natürliche Zahl». logDice: DWDS <https://www.dwds.de/d/wortprofil>: «Abrufbar sind nur Kookkurrenzen, die statistisch signifikant sind, also eine Mindestfrequenz von fünf und einen logDice-Wert größer Null» — corpus is small, so f_xy ≥ 3 here (documented deviation, M-5.5).

## 5. Church Slavonic vs vernacular doublets

Detector rules CS-C1/C3/C4 (core/rules.ts; Шевельов 14.4–14.5) fire only when VESUM attests the East-Slavic counterpart, so each hit is a *pair*. Pairs, with the date of the earliest attestation of each member where the counterpart lemma is in the lexicon:

559 spellings with a church_slavonic label (denominator: 46 510 stored spellings); 108 lemmas with origin = church_slavonic.

| CS spelling | lemma | date | rules | East-Slavic counterpart (VESUM) | counterpart first attested |

## 6. Earliest attestations

Rule P-2.3 / B-3.5: earliest `date.to` among admitted witnesses; ties by vetted > provisional, narrower range, autograph > ms_copy > first_ed > scholarly_ed, person > institution, else co-first. The 30 oldest lemmas of the lexicon (ties on date.to broken by frequency); because the corpus starts with a few 11th-century witnesses, **thousands of lemmas tie for “oldest”** — the date reflects the corpus, not the lexeme’s age (research/06 §3.5).

## 7. Surzhyk / contact and dialect hits

This is a literary corpus; the latest admitted witness is dated 1908. Contact-layer rules are gated to attestations ≥ 1919 (`modern(ctx)`), dialect rules to ≥ 1798 and to forms VESUM does not list as normative (core/detect.ts), so **very few hits are expected, and the ones below are not “surzhyk in the corpus”** — they are lemmas whose headword itself carries a contact label in VESUM/LanguageTool (derived claim, SURZHYK_DEF as evidence).

Surzhyk claims: 423 of 11776 lexicon lemmas. Examples: да (derived: russianism_or_hybrid); імя (derived: russianism_or_hybrid); што (derived: russianism_or_hybrid); оставити (derived: russianism_or_hybrid); около (derived: russianism_or_hybrid); власть (derived: russianism_or_hybrid); враг (derived: russianism_or_hybrid); прочий (derived: russianism_or_hybrid); приняти (derived: russianism_or_hybrid); множество (derived: russianism_or_hybrid); дань (derived: russianism_or_hybrid); грясти (derived: russianism_or_hybrid); грати (derived: russianism_or_hybrid); вон (derived: russianism_or_hybrid); даже (derived: russianism_or_hybrid); украсити (derived: russianism_or_hybrid); бивший (derived: russianism_or_hybrid); память (derived: russianism_or_hybrid); Вкраїна (derived: russianism_or_hybrid); СО (derived: russianism_or_hybrid); далече (derived: russianism_or_hybrid); ад (derived: russianism_or_hybrid); тоже (derived: russianism_or_hybrid); хай (derived: russianism_or_hybrid); чрево (derived: russianism_or_hybrid)

Dialect labels (lemmas): CHN: 222 · MPL: 3 · SE: 4 · POD: 11 · MDN: 10 · BOY: 15 · LEM: 15

**Rusyn axis** (SPEC 40-rusyn, research/10 §5; a parallel reading next to dialect:ZAK/LEM, neutral on language-vs-dialect status — see `rusynStatus` in corpus.json). Gate: only non-normative forms, attestations ≥ 1850. Lemma labels: none; stored spellings with a rusyn claim: 0 of 46510. Rule hits (forms): none. **Expected ≈ 0 here:** the corpus is canonical literary Ukrainian plus Old Rus' / Ruthenian texts; Rusyn spelling (ы after к/г/х, -ськый, ӱ …) would appear only in witnesses by Rusyn-standard authors, and none is admitted. A near-zero count is a property of the corpus, not evidence about Rusyn.

LanguageTool `replace`/`soft` keys found among modern (lang=uk) corpus tokens: 297 types (1 221 tokens). Top: да (235, replace), єсть (193, replace), да (57, replace), тай (46, replace), щож (29, replace), будучи (27, replace), да (22, replace), даже (17, replace), враг (12, replace), куда (9, replace), тай (9, replace), далече (8, replace), получивши (8, soft), государ (8, replace), єсть (8, replace), да (8, replace), власть (8, replace), тоже (7, replace), тута (7, replace), могучий (7, replace). These are 19th-century spellings flagged against *today’s* norm — not evidence of contact.

Python stages: run by this build; stress.json 44 077 forms (ukrainian-word-stress 2.1.0 (data derived from ULIF «Словники України»; licence unclear)); ru.json 4 429 forms (pymorphy3 ru: «known» = present in the OpenCorpora dictionary, not “standard Russian”).

## 8. Caveats

- **Corpus bias.** 1601 witnesses chosen for availability and vetted authorship (research/06 §3.5): chronicles, sermons, a Gospel, early-modern prose, 19th-century poetry. “First attested” means first in *this* corpus.
- **Epochs.** Both schemes are kept apart (research/09 §4). A witness is assigned to the epoch containing its date midpoint for counting; its full assignment (with confidence) is in corpus.json. Epoch boundaries are conventions with fuzz, not events.
- **Model output vs dictionary fact (P-1.7).** The `stanza-lemma` route is a neural lemmatiser trained on TOROT (CC BY-NC-SA 3.0), weak outside its domain (`ruthenian` texts are flagged outOfDomain); its links are labelled with that method and are not dictionary facts. Stress is from `ukrainian-word-stress` (ULIF-derived, licence unclear, context-free) with status `unknown` and method `dictionary`; ru.json is a dictionary-membership test.
- **Frequencies count linked tokens only**; ipm and HK use N = all tokens as denominator, so lemma frequencies are lower bounds by the unresolved share (28.7%).
- **lexicon.json (B-5, core + detail shards)**: lemmas with f ≥ 2 or a pre-1400 attestation: 11 776 of 19 028 lemmas; 11 776 written, none dropped. Core lexicon.json 12.81 MiB (limit 15 MiB), Evidence stored once in a shared pool (27 667 distinct objects; cards carry indices, lossless). Detail (stress, link evidence, full KWIC ≤ 6 per spelling, full statistics) in 2 040 shards lex/<bucket>.json: max 206 KB, median 40 KB, total 87.3 MiB (limit 192 KiB each). lexindex.json 3.65 MB (3319 corpus-attested flagged forms added).
- **Origin claims** are heuristics with citations: `church_slavonic` needs a CS detector hit carrying ≥ half of the lemma’s tokens (no vernacular doublet of comparable weight); `native` needs an orv attestation by torot-lemma/token with only G/X rules in its derivation; otherwise `unknown`. Absence of a label is not evidence of the opposite.
- **Register**: only “colloquial” (from VESUM tags slang/subst/short/vulg) or unknown; “literary” is never inferred (research/08 B3).
- **Collocations** use a ±3 lemma window inside the source segment and a minimum f_xy of 3; linking errors propagate into them.
- **Licences.** VESUM and Brown-UK: CC BY-NC-SA 4.0; TOROT: CC BY-NC-SA 3.0; LanguageTool: LGPL-2.1; pymorphy3-dicts-ru: CC BY-SA; the derived dataset is non-commercial, share-alike (P-4.4).
